- [2026-08-12 21:30] ceo: TRAILING DECISION — KEEP 0.60%. Options A (0.30%) would re-introduce premature exits. Option C (ATR-scaled) adds complexity for 2-3 outlier tokens. B chosen: low-ATR tokens have tight trailing stops because they have low ATR — that's inherent, not a bug. If we want to protect low-ATR tokens, add ATR minimum filter (skip ATR <0.5%), not change trailing distance. No code change. tpsl_utils.py elif fix self-corrects on next position_manager cycle.
- [2026-08-12 20:30] ceo: CEO DECISION NEEDED — TRAILING DISTANCE. TRAILING_DISTANCE_PCT=0.60% is too wide for low-ATR tokens (ADA ATR=0.35%, ASTER ATR=0.17%, LTC ATR=0.22%). Trail_floor = highest * 0.994 ends up ABOVE entry price for tokens where price moved <0.6% from entry to peak. SL gets capped at 1% from entry — still too tight for 0.2% ATR tokens. Options: (A) Reduce to 0.3% — better for low-ATR, may give back profits on high-ATR. (B) Keep 0.6% — low-ATR tokens get tight SLs, that's the nature of low vol. (C) ATR-scaled trailing — max(0.3%, ATR*1.5) — optimal but complex. T: which approach?

- [2026-08-10 19:46] ceo: DECISION — Context gate fix: similar_setup_lookup now runs on ALL paths (not just AMBIGUOUS). Previously signals with speed >= 70% + z confirming direction bypassed all historical WR checks. Now: WR < 30% with n >= 5 → hard SKIP. WR < 50% with n >= 3 → WARN (penalty). - [2026-08-10 20:10] ceo: BUGFIX — hebbian writeback was silently failing on EVERY trade close. Root cause: timezone import missing in hebbian block of _close_trade_impl(). HL lookup imported timezone as _tz2, hebbian used timezone.utc directly → NameError → caught by except:pass → hebbian never learned from trades. Fixed: import timezone as _tz, use _tz.utc. Also: close_trade() now returns result. Also: test fixes. 27/27 tests passing.
